pipeline's accuracy and efficiency.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eUnfortunately, available implementations and published research are yet to realize neural networks' potential. They are hard to use and continuously fail to improve over statistical methods while being computationally prohibitive. class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 holds \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/huggingface/safetensors\"\u003esafetensors\u003c/a\u003e\nweights and JSON metadata, and is loaded without executing any code it contains. Earlier versions\nused pickle, which runs arbitrary code from the artifact on load, so the legacy format is now\nopt-in:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"nf.save('./checkpoints/')                      # writes the new format\nnf2 = NeuralForecast.load('./checkpoints/')    # no pickle, no code execution\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003enf\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003esave\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e'./checkpoints/'\u003c/span\u003e)                      \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e# writes the new format\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003enf2\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003eNeuralForecast\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eload\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e'./checkpoints/'\u003c/span\u003e)    \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e# no pickle, no code execution\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhat changed, if you have artifacts or code from an earlier version:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eOlder directories need consent or conversion.\u003c/strong\u003e Pass \u003ccode\u003eallow_pickle=True\u003c/code\u003e to read one in place,\nwhich executes code contained in it, or convert it once with\n\u003ccode\u003epython -m neuralforecast.migrate ./old_checkpoints/\u003c/code\u003e. A directory saved with\n\u003ccode\u003esave_dataset=True\u003c/code\u003e must be converted — its dataset has no safe reader.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eRemote paths are opt-in.\u003c/strong\u003e \u003ccode\u003eNeuralForecast.load('s3://bucket/models/', trust_remote=True)\u003c/code\u003e,\nbecause whoever can write that location chooses what runs on the loading machine.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCustom losses, optimizers and schedulers must be registered\u003c/strong\u003e with \u003ccode\u003eregister_loss\u003c/code\u003e,\n\u003ccode\u003eregister_optimizer\u003c/code\u003e or \u003ccode\u003eregister_lr_scheduler\u003c/code\u003e, in the process that loads as well as the one\nthat saves.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003eTimeLLM\u003c/code\u003e can no longer be saved or loaded.\u003c/strong\u003e It resolves its \u003ccode\u003ellm\u003c/code\u003e argument through\n\u003ccode\u003efrom_pretrained\u003c/code\u003e while being constructed, so an artifact could direct that fetch. Train and\npredict with it in the same process.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe \u003ca href=\"https://nixtlaverse.nixtla.io/neuralforecast/docs/capabilities/save_load_models.html\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003esave and load guide\u003c/a\u003e\ncovers this in full.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eModels\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-models\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Models\" href=\"#models\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eSee the entire \u003ca href=\"https://nixtlaverse.nixtla.io/neuralforecast/docs/capabilities/overview.html\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003ecollection here\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eMissing a model? <p dir="auto"><a href="#contributors-"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/736decdff0bd6d69d305f31c6e893f012ce8beccc897a6ce790171508004cb2c/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f62616467652f616c6c5f636f6e7472696275746f72732d31312d6f72616e67652e7376673f7374796c653d666c61742d737175617265" alt="All Contributors" data-canonical-src="https://img.shields.io/badge/all_contributors-11-orange.svg?style=flat-square" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>

<p dir="auto"><strong>NeuralForecast</strong> offers a large collection of neural forecasting models focusing on their performance, usability, and robustness. The models range from classic networks like RNNs to the latest transformers: <code>MLP</code>, <code>LSTM</code>, <code>GRU</code>, <code>RNN</code>, <code>TCN</code>, <code>TimesNet</code>, <code>BiTCN</code>, <code>DeepAR</code>, <code>NBEATS</code>, <code>NBEATSx</code>, <code>NHITS</code>, <code>TiDE</code>, <code>DeepNPTS</code>, <code>TSMixer</code>, <code>TSMixerx</code>, <code>MLPMultivariate</code>, <code>DLinear</code>, <code>NLinear</code>, <code>TFT</code>, <code>Informer</code>, <code>AutoFormer</code>, <code>FedFormer</code>, <code>PatchTST</code>, <code>iTransformer</code>, <code>StemGNN</code>, and <code>TimeLLM</code>.</p>
</div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Installation</h2><a id="user-content-installation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Installation" href="#installation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">You can install <code>NeuralForecast</code> with:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="pip install neuralforecast"><pre><span class="pl-s1">pip</span> <span class="pl-s1">install</span> <span class="pl-s1">neuralforecast</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">or</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="conda install -c conda-forge neuralforecast"><pre><span class="pl-s1">conda</span> <span class="pl-s1">install</span> <span class="pl-c1">-</span><span class="pl-s1">c</span> <span class="pl-s1">conda</span><span class="pl-c1">-</span><span class="pl-s1">forge</span> <span class="pl-s1">neuralforecast</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Vist our <a href="https://nixtlaverse.nixtla.io/neuralforecast/docs/getting-started/installation.html" rel="nofollow">Installation Guide</a> for further details.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Quick Start</h2><a id="user-content-quick-start" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Quick Start" href="#quick-start"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Minimal Example</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from neuralforecast import NeuralForecast
from neuralforecast.models import NBEATS
from neuralforecast.utils import AirPassengersDF

nf = NeuralForecast(
    models = [NBEATS(input_size=24, h=12, max_steps=100)],
    freq = 'ME'
)

nf.fit(df=AirPassengersDF)
nf.predict()"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">neuralforecast</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">NeuralForecast</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">neuralforecast</span>.<span class="pl-s1">models</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-c1">NBEATS</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">neuralforecast</span>.<span class="pl-s1">utils</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">AirPassengersDF</span>

<span class="pl-s1">nf</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">NeuralForecast</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">models</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> [<span class="pl-en">NBEATS</span>(<span class="pl-s1">input_size</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">24</span>, <span class="pl-s1">h</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">12</span>, <span class="pl-s1">max_steps</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">100</span>)],
    <span class="pl-s1">freq</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s">'ME'</span>
)

<span class="pl-s1">nf</span>.<span class="pl-c1">fit</span>(<span class="pl-s1">df</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-v">AirPassengersDF</span>)
<span class="pl-s1">nf</span>.<span class="pl-c1">predict</span>()</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Get Started with this <a href="https://nixtlaverse.nixtla.io/neuralforecast/docs/getting-started/quickstart.html" rel="nofollow">quick guide</a>.</strong></p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Why?</h2><a id="user-content-why" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Why?" href="#why"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">There is a shared belief in Neural forecasting methods' capacity to improve forecasting pipeline's accuracy and efficiency.</p>
<p dir="auto">Unfortunately, available implementations and published research are yet to realize neural networks' potential. They are hard to use and continuously fail to improve over statistical methods while being computationally prohibitive. For this reason, we created <code>NeuralForecast</code>, a library favoring proven accurate and efficient models focusing on their usability.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Features</h2><a id="user-content-features" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Features" href="#features"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Fast and accurate implementations of more than 30 state-of-the-art models. See the entire <a href="https://nixtlaverse.nixtla.io/neuralforecast/docs/capabilities/overview.html" rel="nofollow">collection here</a>.</li>
<li>Support for exogenous variables and static covariates.</li>
<li>Interpretability methods for trend, seasonality and exogenous components.</li>
<li>Probabilistic Forecasting with adapters for quantile losses and parametric distributions.</li>
<li>Train and Evaluation Losses with scale-dependent, percentage and scale independent errors, and parametric likelihoods.</li>
<li>Automatic Model Selection with distributed automatic hyperparameter tuning.</li>
<li>Familiar sklearn syntax: <code>.fit</code> and <code>.predict</code>.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Highlights</h2><a id="user-content-highlights" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Highlights" href="#highlights"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Official <code>NHITS</code> implementation, published at AAAI 2023. See <a href="https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/25854" rel="nofollow">paper</a> and <a href="https://github.com/Nixtla/neuralforecast/tree/main/experiments">experiments</a>.</li>
<li>Official <code>NBEATSx</code> implementation, published at the International Journal of Forecasting. See <a href="https://www.sciencedirect.com/science/article/pii/S0169207022000413" rel="nofollow">paper</a>.</li>
<li>Unified with<code>StatsForecast</code>, <code>MLForecast</code>, and <code>HierarchicalForecast</code> interface <code>NeuralForecast().fit(Y_df).predict()</code>, inputs and outputs.</li>
<li>Built-in integrations with <code>utilsforecast</code> and <code>coreforecast</code> for visualization and data-wrangling efficient methods.</li>
<li>Integrations with <code>Ray</code> and <code>Optuna</code> for automatic hyperparameter optimization.</li>
<li>Predict with little to no history using Transfer learning. Check the experiments <a href="https://github.com/Nixtla/transfer-learning-time-series">here</a>.</li>
</ul>

<p dir="auto">📈 <a href="https://nixtlaverse.nixtla.io/neuralforecast/docs/capabilities/hyperparameter_tuning.html" rel="nofollow">Automatic Hyperparameter Optimization</a>: Easy and Scalable Automatic Hyperparameter Optimization with <code>Auto</code> models on <code>Ray</code> or <code>Optuna</code>.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Saving and loading models</h2><a id="user-content-saving-and-loading-models" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Saving and loading models" href="#saving-and-loading-models"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">From 3.3.0 a saved directory holds <a href="https://github.com/huggingface/safetensors">safetensors</a>
weights and JSON metadata, and is loaded without executing any code it contains. Earlier versions
used pickle, which runs arbitrary code from the artifact on load, so the legacy format is now
opt-in:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="nf.save('./checkpoints/')                      # writes the new format
nf2 = NeuralForecast.load('./checkpoints/')    # no pickle, no code execution"><pre><span class="pl-s1">nf</span>.<span class="pl-c1">save</span>(<span class="pl-s">'./checkpoints/'</span>)                      <span class="pl-c"># writes the new format</span>
<span class="pl-s1">nf2</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-v">NeuralForecast</span>.<span class="pl-c1">load</span>(<span class="pl-s">'./checkpoints/'</span>)    <span class="pl-c"># no pickle, no code execution</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">What changed, if you have artifacts or code from an earlier version:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Older directories need consent or conversion.</strong> Pass <code>allow_pickle=True</code> to read one in place,
which executes code contained in it, or convert it once with
<code>python -m neuralforecast.migrate ./old_checkpoints/</code>. A directory saved with
<code>save_dataset=True</code> must be converted — its dataset has no safe reader.</li>
<li><strong>Remote paths are opt-in.</strong> <code>NeuralForecast.load('s3://bucket/models/', trust_remote=True)</code>,
because whoever can write that location chooses what runs on the loading machine.</li>
<li><strong>Custom losses, optimizers and schedulers must be registered</strong> with <code>register_loss</code>,
<code>register_optimizer</code> or <code>register_lr_scheduler</code>, in the process that loads as well as the one
that saves.</li>
<li><strong><code>TimeLLM</code> can no longer be saved or loaded.</strong> It resolves its <code>llm</code> argument through
<code>from_pretrained</code> while being constructed, so an artifact could direct that fetch. Train and
predict with it in the same process.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">The <a href="https://nixtlaverse.nixtla.io/neuralforecast/docs/capabilities/save_load_models.html" rel="nofollow">save and load guide</a>
covers this in full.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Models</h2><a id="user-content-models" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Models" href="#models"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">How to contribute</h2><a id="user-content-how-to-contribute" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: How to contribute" href="#how-to-contribute"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">If you wish to contribute to the project, please refer to our <a href="https://github.com/Nixtla/neuralforecast/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md">contribution guidelines</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">References</h2><a id="user-content-references" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: References" href="#references"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">This work is highly influenced by the fantastic work of previous contributors and other scholars on the neural forecasting methods presented here. We want to highlight the work of <a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/1905.10437" rel="nofollow">Boris Oreshkin</a>, <a href="https://www.sciencedirect.com/science/article/pii/S0169207019301153" rel="nofollow">Slawek Smyl</a>, <a href="https://www.sciencedirect.com/science/article/pii/S0169207021000637" rel="nofollow">Bryan Lim</a>, and <a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/1704.04110" rel="nofollow">David Salinas</a>. We refer to <a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2004.10240" rel="nofollow">Benidis et al.</a> for a comprehensive survey of neural forecasting methods.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🙏 How to cite</h2><a id="user-content--how-to-cite" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🙏 How to cite" href="#-how-to-cite"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">If you enjoy or benefit from using these Python implementations, a citation to the repository will be greatly appreciated.</p>
